    for (const event of uniqueEvents) {
      const eventId = event?.id as string ?? "unknown";
      const comp = (event?.competitions as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)?.[0];
      if (!comp) continue;

      // Get team names for logging
      const competitors = comp?.competitors as Array<Record<string, unknown>> ?? [];
      const home = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === "home");
      const away = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === "away");
      const homeTeam = ((home?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.abbreviation as string) ?? "???";
      const awayTeam = ((away?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.abbreviation as string) ?? "???";

      // Check status
      const statusType = comp?.status as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
      const statusName = (statusType?.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.name as string | undefined;
      const statusState = (statusType?.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.state as string | undefined;
      const statusDesc = ((statusType?.type as Record<string, unknown>)?.description as string | undefined)?.toLowerCase() ?? "";

      // Get game start time for logging
      const compDate = comp?.date as string | undefined;
      const gameTimeStr = compDate ? new Date(compDate).toLocaleTimeString("en-US", { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/New_York" }) + " ET" : "unknown";

      // Skip if status indicates game started or finished
      const startedStatuses = ["STATUS_IN_PROGRESS", "STATUS_FINAL", "STATUS_HALFTIME", "STATUS_END_PERIOD", "STATUS_DELAYED"];
      if (statusName && startedStatuses.includes(statusName)) {
        continue;
      }
      if (statusState === "in" || statusState === "post") {
        continue;
      }

      // Only allow scheduled games
      const isScheduled = statusName === "STATUS_SCHEDULED" || statusDesc.includes("scheduled") || statusState === "pre";
      if (!isScheduled) {
        continue;
      }

      // Time check: skip if scheduled start time is in the past (game has started)
      if (compDate) {
        const gameStart = new Date(compDate);
        if (gameStart < now) {
          continue;
        }
      }

      preGameEvents.push(event);
    }


    // Normalize the input team name for matching (handle "LA" -> "Los Angeles")
    const normalizedTeamForMatch = normalizeTeamName(teamName);

    for (const event of uniqueEvents) {
      for (const comp of (event as Record<string, unknown>)?.competitions as Array<Record<string, unknown>> ?? []) {
        const competitors = comp?.competitors as Array<Record<string, unknown>> ?? [];
        if (competitors.length !== 2) continue;
        const home = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === "home");
        const away = competitors.find((c) => c.homeAway === "away");
        if (!home || !away) continue;

        const homeTeamName = ((home?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.displayName as string ?? "").toLowerCase();
        const awayTeamName = ((away?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.displayName as string ?? "").toLowerCase();
        const homeAbbrev = ((home?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.abbreviation as string ?? "").toLowerCase();
        const awayAbbrev = ((away?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.abbreviation as string ?? "").toLowerCase();

        // Also normalize ESPN team names for comparison
        const homeTeamNormalized = normalizeTeamName(homeTeamName);
        const awayTeamNormalized = normalizeTeamName(awayTeamName);

        // Debug logging for clippers specifically
        if (normalizedTeam.includes("clipper") || homeTeamName.includes("clipper") || awayTeamName.includes("clipper")) {
        }

        const isHomeTeam = homeTeamName.includes(normalizedTeam) || normalizedTeam.includes(homeTeamName) ||
                          homeTeamNormalized === normalizedTeamForMatch || homeAbbrev === normalizedTeam;
        const isAwayTeam = awayTeamName.includes(normalizedTeam) || normalizedTeam.includes(awayTeamName) ||
                          awayTeamNormalized === normalizedTeamForMatch || awayAbbrev === normalizedTeam;

        if (isHomeTeam) {
          const oppId = String((away?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.id ?? "");
          return oppId;
        }
        if (isAwayTeam) {
          const oppId = String((home?.team as Record<string, unknown>)?.id ?? "");
          return oppId;
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  } catch (parseErr) {
    return null;
  }
}

// --- Pick History Logging ---

async function logPickToHistory(result: PropAnalysisResult, prop: ExtractedProp, recommendationShown: boolean, aiAnalysis: string | null = null): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Calculate game_date in Eastern time (EDT = UTC-4)
    const now = new Date();
    const eastern = new Date(now.getTime() - 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const gameDate = eastern.toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, "");

    const pickHistoryRow = {
      player_name: result.playerName,
      team: result.team,
      opponent: normalizeTeamName(prop.homeTeam) === normalizeTeamName(result.team) ? prop.awayTeam : prop.homeTeam,
      game_time: formatGameTime(prop.gameTime),
      game_date: gameDate,
      is_home: result.isHome ?? null,
      prop_type: result.propType,
      line: result.line,
      pick_side: result.pickSide,
      odds: result.odds,
      season_avg: result.seasonAvg ?? null,
      recent_avg: result.recentAvg ?? null,
      floor_val: result.floor ?? null,
      ceiling_val: result.ceiling ?? null,
      l5_hit_count: result.hitRatesRaw?.l5Hits ?? null,
      l10_hit_count: result.hitRatesRaw?.l10Hits ?? null,
      season_hit_pct: result.hitRatesRaw?.seasonRate ?? null,
      is_b2b: result.isBackToBack ?? false,
      rest_days: result.restDays ?? null,
      minutes_l5_avg: result.minutesTrend?.l5Avg ?? null,
      minutes_l10_avg: result.minutesTrend?.l10Avg ?? null,
      minutes_trend: result.minutesTrend?.direction ?? null,
      opp_ppg_allowed: result.oppStats?.pointsAllowedPerGame ?? null,
      opp_rpg_allowed: result.oppStats?.reboundsAllowedPerGame ?? null,
      opp_fg_pct_allowed: result.oppStats?.oppFieldGoalPct ?? null,
      opp_3pt_pct_allowed: result.oppStats?.oppThreePointPct ?? null,
      pace_opp_ppg: result.oppStats?.pointsAllowedPerGame ?? null,
      score_l5: result.breakdown?.l5HitRate ?? 0,
      score_l10: result.breakdown?.l10HitRate ?? 0,
      score_season: result.breakdown?.seasonHitRate ?? 0,
      score_floor_ceiling: result.breakdown?.floorCeiling ?? 0,
      score_recent_form: result.breakdown?.recentForm ?? 0,
      score_home_away: result.breakdown?.homeAway ?? 0,
      score_rest: result.breakdown?.restDays ?? 0,
      score_b2b: result.breakdown?.backToBack ?? 0,
      score_minutes_trend: result.breakdown?.minutesTrend ?? 0,
      score_pace: result.breakdown?.pace ?? 0,
      score_opp_defense: result.breakdown?.opponentDefense ?? 0,
      score_odds_value: result.breakdown?.oddsValue ?? 0,
      score_z_score: result.breakdown?.zScoreBonus ?? 0,
      score_role_change: result.breakdown?.roleChangeBonus ?? 0,
      score_vig_filter: result.breakdown?.vigFilterPenalty ?? 0,
      score_usg_rate: result.breakdown?.usgBonus ?? 0,
      score_regression: result.breakdown?.regressionBonus ?? 0,
      score_market_conf: result.breakdown?.marketConfBonus ?? 0,
      score_home_away_split: result.breakdown?.homeAwaySplitBonus ?? 0,
      score_minutes_floor: result.breakdown?.minutesFloorBonus ?? 0,
      score_consistency: result.breakdown?.consistencyBonus ?? 0,
      score_prop_type_penalty: result.breakdown?.propTypePenalty ?? 0,
      score_stale_data: result.breakdown?.staleDataPenalty ?? 0,
      score_player_injury: result.breakdown?.playerInjuryPenalty ?? 0,
      projected_stat: (result as any).projectionData?.projectedStat ?? null,
      stat_stdev: (result as any).projectionData?.statStdDev ?? null,
      z_score: (result as any).projectionData?.zScore ?? null,
      per_minute_rate: (result as any).projectionData?.perMinRate ?? null,
      projected_minutes: (result as any).projectionData?.projectedMinutes ?? null,
      teammate_injuries_count: (result as any).projectionData?.teammateInjuriesCount ?? null,
      usage_boost: (result as any).projectionData?.usageBoost ?? null,
      confidence: result.confidence,
      verdict: result.verdict,
      ai_analysis: aiAnalysis,
      source: "dashboard",
      recommendation_shown: recommendationShown,
    };

    const logUrl = `${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")}/rest/v1/pick_history`;
    const logHeaders = {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "apikey": Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "",
      "Authorization": `Bearer ${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? ""}`,
      "Prefer": "return=minimal,resolution=ignore-duplicates",
    };

    await fetch(logUrl, { method: "POST", headers: logHeaders, body: JSON.stringify(pickHistoryRow) });
  } catch (parseErr) { /* ignore logging errors */ }
}

// --- Player Data Fetching ---

async function fetchPlayerDataCached(playerName: string): Promise<CachedPlayerData | null> {
  // Check DB cache first
  const SUPA_URL = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") || "";
  const SUPA_KEY = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") || "";
  const EDT_OFF = 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const cacheDate = new Date(Date.now() - EDT_OFF).toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, "");

  try {
    const cacheUrl = SUPA_URL + "/rest/v1/player_data_cache?player_name=eq." + encodeURIComponent(playerName) + "&cache_date=eq." + cacheDate + "&select=data&limit=1";
    const cacheRes = await fetch(cacheUrl, { headers: { "apikey": SUPA_KEY, "Authorization": "Bearer " + SUPA_KEY } });
    if (cacheRes.ok) {
      const rows = await cacheRes.json();
      if (rows.length > 0 && rows[0].data) {
        return rows[0].data as CachedPlayerData;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { /* cache miss, fetch live */ }

  // Cache miss — fetch from ESPN
  const data = await fetchPlayerDataWithTimeout(playerName);

  // Store in cache (fire and forget)
  if (data) {
    try {
      fetch(SUPA_URL + "/rest/v1/player_data_cache", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "apikey": SUPA_KEY, "Authorization": "Bearer " + SUPA_KEY, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Prefer": "resolution=merge-duplicates" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ player_name: playerName, cache_date: cacheDate, data }),
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-critical */ }
  }
  return data;
}

async function fetchPlayerDataWithTimeout(playerName: string): Promise<CachedPlayerData | null> {
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt++) {
    const timeoutPromise = new Promise<null>((resolve) => {
      setTimeout(() => resolve(null), ESPN_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS);
    });
    const fetchPromise = (async (): Promise<CachedPlayerData | null> => {
      const player = await searchPlayer(playerName, "basketball", ESPN_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS);
      if (!player) return null;
      const gameLog = await fetchGameLog(player.id, "basketball", ESPN_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS);
      if (!gameLog.length) return null;
      const minutesTrend = calculateMinutesTrend(gameLog);
      return { player, gameLog, minutesTrend };
    })();
    const result = await Promise.race([fetchPromise, timeoutPromise]);
    if (result) return result;
    if (attempt === 1) console.log("[espn] Retry for " + playerName);
  }
  return null;
}

// --- Score a single prop ---

async function scoreProp(
  playerData: CachedPlayerData,
  prop: ExtractedProp,
  edgeData: { b2b: { isBackToBack: boolean; restDays: number }; oppStats: OpponentStats | null }
): PropAnalysisResult | null {
  // Score both OVER and UNDER, return the better pick
  const overResult = await scoreOneSide(playerData, prop, edgeData, "over");
  const underResult = await scoreOneSide(playerData, prop, edgeData, "under");
  
  if (!overResult && !underResult) return null;
  if (!overResult) return underResult;
  if (!underResult) return overResult;
  
  // Return whichever side has higher confidence
  if (underResult.confidence > overResult.confidence) {
    console.log("[side] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | " + prop.propType + " | UNDER=" + underResult.confidence + " > OVER=" + overResult.confidence + " — picking UNDER");
    return underResult;
  }
  return overResult;
}

async function scoreOneSide(
  playerData: CachedPlayerData,
  prop: ExtractedProp,
  edgeData: { b2b: { isBackToBack: boolean; restDays: number }; oppStats: OpponentStats | null },
  pickSide: "over" | "under"
): PropAnalysisResult | null {
  const { player, gameLog, minutesTrend } = playerData;

  const hitRates = calcHitRates(gameLog, prop.propType, prop.line, pickSide);
  if (!hitRates.values.length) return null;

  const allValues = hitRates.values;

  // Prevents inflated scores from tiny samples (e.g. Scoot Henderson 100 conf on 1 game)
  if (allValues.length < 5) {
    console.log(`[score] SKIP ${playerData.player.displayName} | ${prop.propType} | only ${allValues.length} games (min 5 required)`);
    return null;
  }

  const recentWindow = allValues.slice(0, 10);
  const playerFloor = Math.min(...recentWindow);
  const playerCeiling = Math.max(...recentWindow);
  const seasonAvg = allValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / allValues.length;
  const recentValues = allValues.slice(0, 5);
  const recentAvg = recentValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / recentValues.length;
  const isHome = prop.homeTeam === player.team || normalizeTeamName(prop.homeTeam) === normalizeTeamName(player.team);

  // Detect recent absences from game log
  const absenceInfo = detectRecentAbsence(gameLog);

  const { paceScore, defenseScore } = calculatePaceDefenseScores(edgeData.oppStats, prop.propType, "over");

  // === PROJECTION ENGINE (#104-#107) ===
  const statStdDev = calculateStdDev(allValues.slice(0, 10));
  const perMinRate = calculatePerMinuteRate(gameLog, prop.propType);
  const projMins = projectMinutes(gameLog, edgeData.b2b.isBackToBack);
  const rawProjected = computeProjectedStat(perMinRate, projMins, paceScore);
  const teamInjuriesArr = await getTeamInjuries(player.team);
  const usageBoostResult = calculateUsageBoost(teamInjuriesArr, projMins);
  const projectedStat = Math.round(rawProjected * (1 + usageBoostResult.boostPct) * 100) / 100;
  const zScore = calculateZScore(projectedStat, prop.line, statStdDev, "over");
  if (zScore !== 0) {
    console.log("[proj] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | " + prop.propType + " O" + prop.line + " | proj=" + projectedStat.toFixed(1) + " | stdev=" + statStdDev + " | z=" + zScore + " | perMin=" + perMinRate + " | mins=" + projMins + " | injuries=" + usageBoostResult.injuredCount + " boost=" + (usageBoostResult.boostPct*100).toFixed(0) + "%");
  }

  // === #119: Role Change Detection ===
  const roleChange = detectRoleChange(gameLog);
  if (roleChange.detected) {
    console.log("[role] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | " + roleChange.direction.toUpperCase() + " | L3=" + roleChange.l3Avg + "min vs L10=" + roleChange.l10Avg + "min (" + (roleChange.pctChange > 0 ? "+" : "") + roleChange.pctChange + "%)");
  }

  // === #126: Minutes Floor Detection ===
  const minsFloor = detectMinutesFloor(gameLog);
  if (minsFloor.isStable) {
    console.log("[mins] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | STABLE role | min=" + minsFloor.minMinutes + " max=" + minsFloor.maxMinutes + " spread=" + minsFloor.spread);
  }
  if (minsFloor.isVolatile) {
    console.log("[mins] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | VOLATILE role | min=" + minsFloor.minMinutes + " max=" + minsFloor.maxMinutes + " spread=" + minsFloor.spread);
  }

  // === #113: Home/Away Splits ===
  const hasSplit = calculateHomeAwaySplit(gameLog, prop.propType, isHome);
  if (Math.abs(hasSplit.edgePct) >= 10) {
    console.log("[split] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | " + (isHome ? "HOME" : "AWAY") + " avg=" + hasSplit.splitAvg + " vs opposite=" + hasSplit.oppSplitAvg + " (" + (hasSplit.edgePct > 0 ? "+" : "") + hasSplit.edgePct + "%)");
  }

  // === #123: Hot Streak Anti-Signal ===
  const marketConf = detectMarketConfirmation(hitRates.l5.rate, hitRates.l10.rate);
  if (marketConf.isOverpriced) {
    console.log("[mkt] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | OVERPRICED — L5 100% but L10 < 80%, line likely moved up");
  }
  if (marketConf.isColdBuy) {
    console.log("[mkt] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | COLD BUY — L5 ≤20% but L10 ≥50%, line may not have dropped");
  }

  // === #117: Regression to Mean ===
  const regression = detectRegression(recentAvg, seasonAvg, prop.line, pickSide);
  if (regression.signal !== "none") {
    console.log("[regr] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | " + regression.signal.toUpperCase() + " | L5 vs Season: " + regression.pctDiff + "%");
  }

  // === #122: Usage Rate ===
  const usgRate = calculateUSGRate(gameLog);
  if (usgRate > 0) {
    console.log("[usg] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | USG=" + usgRate + "%");
  }

  const confidenceResult = calculateConfidenceScore({
    l5HitRate: hitRates.l5.rate,
    l10HitRate: hitRates.l10.rate,
    seasonHitRate: hitRates.season.rate,
    line: prop.line,
    playerFloor,
    playerCeiling,
    recentAvg,
    seasonAvg,
    isHome,
    odds: prop.odds,
    pickSide: pickSide,
    propType: prop.propType,
    restDays: edgeData.b2b.restDays,
    isBackToBack: edgeData.b2b.isBackToBack,
    minutesTrendScore: minutesTrend.score,
    paceScore,
    defenseScore,
  });

  // === Z-SCORE CONFIDENCE ADJUSTMENT (#106) ===
  let finalScore = confidenceResult.score;
  let zScoreBonus = 0;
  if (zScore >= 1.5) zScoreBonus = 8;
  else if (zScore >= 1.0) zScoreBonus = 5;
  else if (zScore >= 0.5) zScoreBonus = 2;
  else if (zScore <= -1.0) zScoreBonus = -8;
  else if (zScore <= -0.5) zScoreBonus = -4;
  finalScore += Math.round(zScoreBonus * WEIGHTS.zScore);

  // === CONSISTENCY BONUS/PENALTY ===
  let consistencyBonus = 0;
  if (statStdDev > 0) {
    const coeffOfVariation = statStdDev / (projectedStat || 1);
    if (coeffOfVariation <= 0.15) consistencyBonus = 4;
    else if (coeffOfVariation <= 0.25) consistencyBonus = 2;
    else if (coeffOfVariation >= 0.50) consistencyBonus = -5;
    else if (coeffOfVariation >= 0.40) consistencyBonus = -3;
  }
  finalScore += Math.round(consistencyBonus * WEIGHTS.consistency);

  // === #119: ROLE CHANGE BONUS/PENALTY ===
  let roleChangeBonus = 0;
  if (roleChange.detected) {
    if (roleChange.direction === "promotion") roleChangeBonus = 5;
    else if (roleChange.direction === "demotion") roleChangeBonus = -6;
  }
  finalScore += Math.round(roleChangeBonus * WEIGHTS.roleChange);

  // === #125: MINIMUM EDGE THRESHOLD (VIG FILTER) ===
  // If projected stat is within 5% of line and z-score is weak, penalize — not enough edge to beat vig
  let vigFilterPenalty = 0;
  if (projectedStat > 0 && prop.line > 0) {
    const edgePct = Math.abs(projectedStat - prop.line) / prop.line;
    if (edgePct < 0.05 && Math.abs(zScore) < 0.5) {
      vigFilterPenalty = -6;
      console.log("[vig] " + playerData.player.displayName + " | " + prop.propType + " | proj=" + projectedStat.toFixed(1) + " vs line=" + prop.line + " | edge=" + (edgePct*100).toFixed(1) + "% | z=" + zScore + " | FILTERED -6");
    }
  }
  finalScore += Math.round(vigFilterPenalty * WEIGHTS.vigFilter);

  // === #122: USG RATE BONUS ===
  // High-usage players are more predictable for overs. Low-usage = fewer touches = volatile.
  let usgBonus = 0;
  if (usgRate >= 30) usgBonus = 3;        // Star usage (Luka, Jokic tier)
  else if (usgRate >= 25) usgBonus = 1;   // Above average
  else if (usgRate > 0 && usgRate < 15) usgBonus = -3;  // Very low usage, unreliable for overs
  finalScore += Math.round(usgBonus * WEIGHTS.usgRate);

  // === #117: REGRESSION TO MEAN BONUS ===
  let regressionBonus = 0;
  if (regression.signal === "buy_low") regressionBonus = 4;     // Cold streak = bounce back expected
  else if (regression.signal === "sell_high") regressionBonus = -4; // Hot streak = regression down likely
  finalScore += Math.round(regressionBonus * WEIGHTS.regression);

  // === #123: HOT STREAK ANTI-SIGNAL ===
  let marketConfBonus = 0;
  if (marketConf.isOverpriced) marketConfBonus = -4;  // Line already moved, edge gone
  if (marketConf.isColdBuy) marketConfBonus = 3;      // Market slow to drop, buy the dip
  finalScore += Math.round(marketConfBonus * WEIGHTS.marketConf);

  // === #113: HOME/AWAY SPLIT BONUS ===
  let homeAwaySplitBonus = 0;
  if (Math.abs(hasSplit.edgePct) >= 15) {
    homeAwaySplitBonus = hasSplit.edgePct > 0 ? 3 : -3;
  } else if (Math.abs(hasSplit.edgePct) >= 10) {
    homeAwaySplitBonus = hasSplit.edgePct > 0 ? 2 : -2;
  }
  finalScore += Math.round(homeAwaySplitBonus * WEIGHTS.haSplit);

  // === #126: MINUTES FLOOR BONUS ===
  let minutesFloorBonus = 0;
  if (minsFloor.isStable) minutesFloorBonus = 3;
  if (minsFloor.isVolatile) minutesFloorBonus = -4;
  finalScore += Math.round(minutesFloorBonus * WEIGHTS.minutesFloor);

  // === #133: STALE DATA DETECTION ===
  // Two checks: (A) days since last game vs today, (B) gap between last 2 games
  // Threshold at 14+ days avoids All-Star break false positives (~8 day break)
  let staleDataPenalty = 0;
  if (gameLog.length > 0 && gameLog[0].date) {
    const lastGameDate = new Date(gameLog[0].date);
    const now = new Date();
    const daysSinceLastGame = Math.floor((now.getTime() - lastGameDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));

    // (A) Player hasn't played in 14+ days — extended absence (scaled)
    if (daysSinceLastGame >= 35) {
      staleDataPenalty = -30;
      console.log("[stale] " + player.displayName + " | " + daysSinceLastGame + " days since last game | penalty=" + staleDataPenalty);
    } else if (daysSinceLastGame >= 21) {
      staleDataPenalty = -20;
      console.log("[stale] " + player.displayName + " | " + daysSinceLastGame + " days since last game | penalty=" + staleDataPenalty);
    } else if (daysSinceLastGame >= 14) {
      staleDataPenalty = -10;
      console.log("[stale] " + player.displayName + " | " + daysSinceLastGame + " days since last game | penalty=" + staleDataPenalty);
    }

    // (B) Just returned — gap between last 2 games means L5 data spans an absence
    if (staleDataPenalty === 0 && gameLog.length >= 2 && gameLog[1].date) {
      const prevDate = new Date(gameLog[1].date);
      const topGapDays = Math.floor((lastGameDate.getTime() - prevDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
      if (topGapDays >= 14) {
        staleDataPenalty = -10;
        console.log("[stale] " + player.displayName + " | just returned, " + topGapDays + "-day gap in L5 | penalty=" + staleDataPenalty);
      }
    }
  }
  finalScore += Math.round(staleDataPenalty * WEIGHTS.staleData);

  // === #132: PLAYER INJURY STATUS CHECK (BallDontLie) ===
  let playerInjuryPenalty = 0;
  const injuryStatus = getPlayerInjuryStatus(player.displayName, player.team);
  if (injuryStatus.isInjured) {
    playerInjuryPenalty = injuryStatus.penalty;
    console.log("[injury-self] " + player.displayName + " | " + injuryStatus.status + " | penalty=" + playerInjuryPenalty);
  }
  finalScore += Math.round(playerInjuryPenalty * WEIGHTS.playerInjury);

  // Re-clamp after penalties
  finalScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, finalScore));

  return {
    playerName: player.displayName,
    team: player.team,
    propType: prop.propType.replace("player_", ""),
    line: prop.line,
    pickSide: pickSide,
    odds: prop.odds,
    confidence: finalScore,
    verdict: getScoreLabel(confidenceResult.score),
    hitRates: {
      l5: `${Math.round(hitRates.l5.rate)}%`,
      l10: `${Math.round(hitRates.l10.rate)}%`,
      season: `${Math.round(hitRates.season.rate)}%`,
    },
    hitRatesRaw: { l5Hits: hitRates.l5.hits, l10Hits: hitRates.l10.hits, seasonRate: hitRates.season.rate },
    seasonAvg: Math.round(seasonAvg * 100) / 100,
    recentAvg: Math.round(recentAvg * 100) / 100,
    floor: playerFloor,
    ceiling: playerCeiling,
    last5Values: recentValues,
    isHome,
    isBackToBack: edgeData.b2b.isBackToBack,
    restDays: edgeData.b2b.restDays,
    minutesTrend,
    oppStats: edgeData.oppStats,
    breakdown: { ...confidenceResult.breakdown, zScoreBonus, consistencyBonus, roleChangeBonus, vigFilterPenalty, usgBonus, usgRate, regressionBonus, marketConfBonus, homeAwaySplitBonus, minutesFloorBonus, staleDataPenalty, playerInjuryPenalty, projectedStat, zScore: zScore, statStdDev },
    absenceInfo,
    projectionData: { projectedStat, statStdDev, zScore, perMinRate, projectedMinutes: projMins, teammateInjuriesCount: usageBoostResult.injuredCount, usageBoost: usageBoostResult.boostPct },
  };
}

// --- Gemini AI Analysis ---

async function getGeminiAnalysis(data: {
  playerName: string;
  team: string;
  propType: string;
  pickSide: string;
  line: number;
  odds: number;
  seasonAvg: number;
  recentAvg: number;
  floor: number;
  ceiling: number;
  confidence: number;
  last5Values: number[];
  hitRates: { l5: string; l10: string };
  isHome: boolean;
  opponent?: string;
  oppStats?: { pointsAllowedPerGame: number; reboundsAllowedPerGame: number; assistsAllowedPerGame: number; defenseRank: string } | null;
  absenceInfo?: AbsenceInfo | null;
  teamInjuries?: string[];
  opponentInjuries?: string[];
}): Promise<string | null> {
  const last5Str = data.last5Values.join(", ");
  const homeAway = data.isHome ? "HOME" : "AWAY";

  // Build absence line if player had a recent absence
  const absenceLine = data.absenceInfo
    ? `- RECENT ABSENCE: Player missed approximately ${data.absenceInfo.gamesEstimate} games (${data.absenceInfo.daysGap}-day gap between ${data.absenceInfo.fromDate} and ${data.absenceInfo.toDate})`
    : "";

  // Build injury sections
  const teamInjuriesStr = data.teamInjuries && data.teamInjuries.length > 0
    ? data.teamInjuries.join("\n  ")
    : "None reported";
  const oppInjuriesStr = data.opponentInjuries && data.opponentInjuries.length > 0
    ? data.opponentInjuries.join("\n  ")
    : "None reported";

  // Determine opponent stat value and label based on prop type
  const normalizedProp = data.propType.replace("player_", "").toLowerCase();
  let oppStatValue = 0;
  let oppStatLabel = "PPG";
  if (data.oppStats) {
    if (normalizedProp === "points") {
      oppStatValue = data.oppStats.pointsAllowedPerGame;
      oppStatLabel = "PPG allowed";
    } else {
      // ESPN APG/RPG 'allowed' are actually the team's OWN stats, not opponent-allowed
      // Don't pass bad data to Gemini — only PPG is reliable
      oppStatValue = 0;
      oppStatLabel = "";
    }
  }

  const prompt = `You are a sharp sports betting analyst. Your job is to give a concise 2-3 sentence analysis that tells the bettor something they CAN'T already see from the raw stats. Focus on WHY this pick is good or risky, not WHAT the numbers are. Never just list the last 5 game values — the user already sees those. Be direct and opinionated.

MANDATORY DATA (reference only, do not restate these to the user):
- Player: ${data.playerName} (${data.team})
- Prop: ${data.propType} ${data.pickSide} ${data.line}
- Last 5 game values: [${last5Str}]
- Season avg: ${data.seasonAvg} | Recent avg: ${data.recentAvg}
- Hit rate L5: ${data.hitRates.l5} | L10: ${data.hitRates.l10}
- Floor: ${data.floor} | Ceiling: ${data.ceiling}
- ${data.playerName} plays FOR ${data.team}. They are playing AGAINST ${data.opponent ?? "Unknown"} (${homeAway}).
- ${data.opponent ?? "Unknown"} DEFENSE (the team ${data.playerName} is facing): ${oppStatValue > 0 ? "allows " + oppStatValue.toFixed(1) + " " + oppStatLabel : "no reliable defensive stat available for this prop type"} (${data.oppStats?.defenseRank ?? "unknown"})
- IMPORTANT: Do NOT reference ${data.team} defensive stats. ${data.playerName} is ON ${data.team}, not playing against them.
${absenceLine}

TEAMMATE INJURIES (${data.team}):
  ${teamInjuriesStr}

OPPONENT INJURIES (${data.opponent ?? "Unknown"}):
  ${oppInjuriesStr}

Based on the data above, write a 2-3 sentence betting analysis. You MUST follow these rules:

1. CRITICAL: The algorithm picked the ${data.pickSide.toUpperCase()} (${data.pickSide === "over" ? "player EXCEEDS" : "player STAYS BELOW"} ${data.line} ${normalizedProp}). Your analysis MUST argue for or critique the ${data.pickSide.toUpperCase()} direction. NEVER argue for the opposite side. If the pick is UNDER, explain why the player will stay below the line. If OVER, explain why they will exceed it.
2. Do NOT restate the last 5 values, season average, or hit rates — the user already sees these on the dashboard.
3. DO analyze: momentum/trends (hot streak vs cooling off), the matchup (what makes ${data.opponent ?? "this opponent"} good/bad for ${normalizedProp} specifically), any red flags (volatile floor, minute restrictions, absence return), and whether the line feels soft or tight relative to recent performance.
4. If RECENT ABSENCE is flagged, lead with that — it's the most important factor.
5. Reference ${data.opponent ?? "the opponent"} by name and say something specific about them defensively (not just 'they allow X ${oppStatLabel}').
6. End with TAKE, LEAN, or FADE the ${data.pickSide.toUpperCase()} — not just TAKE every time. LEAN means slight edge but risky. FADE means the numbers look good but something concerns you.
7. Keep it to 2-3 sentences max. Be sharp, not verbose.
8. Quote the correct opponent stat for this prop type (${oppStatLabel} for ${normalizedProp}). Do not cite PPG for an assists prop.
9. IMPORTANT: Players listed as "Suspended" or who have been out ALL SEASON are NOT game-day factors. Do not say they are "out" as if it changes tonight's game — the team has been playing without them for months. Only reference injuries/absences that are RECENT (within the last few weeks).
10. If key teammates have a RECENT injury (Out or Day-To-Day within the last few weeks), consider how their absence affects the player's usage rate, shot attempts, or role.

No markdown formatting, no asterisks, no disclaimers.`;

  const apiUrl = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${GEMINI_API_KEY}`;
  const requestBody = {
    contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }],
    generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: 200, temperature: 0.7 },
  };

  async function makeRequest(): Promise<{ status: number; text: string }> {
    const response = await fetch(apiUrl, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(requestBody),
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    return { status: response.status, text };
  }

  try {
    let result = await makeRequest();

    if (result.status === 429) {
      console.log(`[gemini] Rate limited, waiting 3s and retrying...`);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
      result = await makeRequest();
    }

    if (result.status !== 200) {
      console.log(`[gemini] API error: ${result.status}`);
      return null;
    }

    const json = JSON.parse(result.text);
    const text = json?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
    if (text) {
      return text.replace(/\*\*/g, '').replace(/\*/g, '').replace(/##/g, '').replace(/#/g, '').trim();
    }
    return null;
  } catch (err) {
    console.log(`[gemini] Exception: ${err}`);
    return null;
  }
}

// --- Game Pick AI Analysis ---

async function getGameGeminiAnalysis(data: {
  pickType: string;
  homeTeam: string;
  awayTeam: string;
  pickSide: string;
  line: number;
  odds: number;
  confidence: number;
  breakdown: Record<string, number>;
  homeInjuries: string[];
  awayInjuries: string[];
}): Promise<string | null> {
  const sideLabel = data.pickType === "spread"
    ? (data.pickSide === "home" ? data.homeTeam + " " + (data.line > 0 ? "+" : "") + data.line : data.awayTeam + " " + (data.line > 0 ? "+" : "") + (-data.line))
    : (data.pickSide === "over" ? "Over " + data.line : "Under " + data.line);

  const homeInjStr = data.homeInjuries.length > 0 ? data.homeInjuries.join(", ") : "None reported";
  const awayInjStr = data.awayInjuries.length > 0 ? data.awayInjuries.join(", ") : "None reported";

  const prompt = `You are a sharp NBA game betting analyst. Give a concise 2-3 sentence analysis for this ${data.pickType === "spread" ? "spread" : "total"} pick.

MATCHUP: ${data.awayTeam} @ ${data.homeTeam}
PICK: ${sideLabel} (${data.odds > 0 ? "+" : ""}${data.odds})
CONFIDENCE: ${data.confidence}/100
SCORING BREAKDOWN: ${JSON.stringify(data.breakdown)}

${data.homeTeam} INJURIES: ${homeInjStr}
${data.awayTeam} INJURIES: ${awayInjStr}

Rules:
1. CRITICAL: Argue for or critique the ${sideLabel} pick. Do NOT argue for the opposite side.
2. Factor in coaching tendencies and offensive/defensive schemes — pace of play, defensive philosophy, tendency to go big or small, half-court vs transition offense. You know NBA coaches — reference them by name if relevant.
3. Consider how injuries change rotation, pace, and scoring distribution — not just "star X is out" but HOW it changes the game flow.
4. For totals: analyze pace matchup, both teams' recent scoring trends, and whether the game environment favors high or low scoring.
5. For spreads: analyze which team has the better current form, matchup advantages, and home court factor.
6. End with TAKE, LEAN, or FADE. Be honest — if the edge is thin, say LEAN or FADE.
7. Keep it to 2-3 sentences. Be sharp and opinionated.
8. Players who have been suspended or out ALL SEASON are not game-day factors. Do not reference them.

No markdown formatting, no asterisks, no disclaimers.`;

  const apiUrl = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=" + GEMINI_API_KEY;
  const requestBody = { contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }], generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: 200, temperature: 0.7 } };

  try {
    let res = await fetch(apiUrl, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(requestBody) });
    let text = await res.text();
    if (res.status === 429) {
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
      const retry = await fetch(apiUrl, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(requestBody) });
      text = await retry.text();
      if (retry.status !== 200) return null;
    } else if (res.status !== 200) return null;

    const json = JSON.parse(text);
    const result = json?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
    return result ? result.replace(/\*\*/g, "").replace(/\*/g, "").replace(/##/g, "").replace(/#/g, "").trim() : null;
  } catch (err) {
    console.log("[gemini-game] Error: " + err);
    return null;
  }
}

// --- Context Modifiers (AI-powered adjustments) ---

async function getContextModifiers(data: {
  playerName: string;
  propType: string;
  line: number;
  pickSide: string;
  baseConfidence: number;
  seasonAvg: number;
  recentAvg: number;
  last5Values: number[];
  teamInjuries: string[];
  opponentInjuries: string[];
  absenceInfo: string;
  opponentName: string;
}): Promise<{ modifiers: Record<string, number>; reasons: Record<string, string>; totalAdjustment: number }> {

  const prompt = `You are a sports betting context analyst. Given a player prop pick that has been scored by an algorithm, analyze contextual factors and return JSON score adjustments.

PLAYER: ${data.playerName}
PROP: ${data.propType} ${data.pickSide} ${data.line}
BASE CONFIDENCE: ${data.baseConfidence}
SEASON AVG: ${data.seasonAvg} | RECENT AVG: ${data.recentAvg}
LAST 5 VALUES: ${JSON.stringify(data.last5Values)}
OPPONENT: ${data.opponentName}

TEAMMATE INJURIES: ${data.teamInjuries.length > 0 ? data.teamInjuries.join('; ') : 'None reported'}
OPPONENT INJURIES: ${data.opponentInjuries.length > 0 ? data.opponentInjuries.join('; ') : 'None reported'}
ABSENCE STATUS: ${data.absenceInfo}

Return ONLY a JSON object with these fields (no markdown, no backticks, no explanation):
{
  "usageShift": <integer -5 to +5, positive if player likely gets MORE usage due to teammate injuries/absences>,
  "usageReason": "<one sentence why>",
  "absenceRust": <integer -5 to 0, negative if player returning from absence and may have rust>,
  "absenceReason": "<one sentence why>",
  "matchupEdge": <integer -3 to +3, positive if opponent injuries/weakness create favorable matchup>,
  "matchupReason": "<one sentence why>",
  "volatilityRisk": <integer -3 to 0, negative if last 5 values show extreme inconsistency>,
  "volatilityReason": "<one sentence why>"
}

RULES:
- Each modifier MUST be within its stated range
- If no relevant context exists for a modifier, set it to 0
- Be conservative — only non-zero if there's clear evidence
- usageShift should be positive when key teammates (starters, high-usage players) are Out
- absenceRust should be negative only if ABSENCE STATUS indicates a recent return
- volatilityRisk: calculate the standard deviation of last 5 values relative to the line. If std dev > 40% of the line, apply -2 or -3`;

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }],
        generationConfig: { temperature: 0.1, maxOutputTokens: 300 }
      })
    });

    const result = await response.json();
    const text = result.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '{}';
    const cleaned = text.replace(/```json\n?/g, '').replace(/```\n?/g, '').trim();
    const parsed = JSON.parse(cleaned);

    // Clamp each modifier to its range
    const modifiers = {
      usageShift: Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, parsed.usageShift || 0)),
      absenceRust: Math.max(-5, Math.min(0, parsed.absenceRust || 0)),
      matchupEdge: Math.max(-3, Math.min(3, parsed.matchupEdge || 0)),
      volatilityRisk: Math.max(-3, Math.min(0, parsed.volatilityRisk || 0))
    };

    const reasons = {
      usageShift: parsed.usageReason || '',
      absenceRust: parsed.absenceReason || '',
      matchupEdge: parsed.matchupReason || '',
      volatilityRisk: parsed.volatilityReason || ''
    };

    let totalAdjustment = Object.values(modifiers).reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0);
    // Cap total adjustment at ±10
    totalAdjustment = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, totalAdjustment));

    console.log(`[context-mod] ${data.playerName} | base=${data.baseConfidence} | mods=${JSON.stringify(modifiers)} | total=${totalAdjustment} | final=${data.baseConfidence + totalAdjustment}`);

    return { modifiers, reasons, totalAdjustment };
  } catch (err) {
    console.log(`[context-mod] ERROR for ${data.playerName}: ${err}`);
    return { modifiers: {}, reasons: {}, totalAdjustment: 0 };
  }
}

// --- Main Handler ---


// ============================================================
// GAME SIDES & TOTALS (#59) — Team-level betting analysis
// ============================================================

interface GameOdds {
  eventId: string;
  homeTeam: string;
  awayTeam: string;
  gameTime: string;
  spread?: { homeSpread: number; homeOdds: number; awaySpread: number; awayOdds: number };
  total?: { line: number; overOdds: number; underOdds: number };
}

interface TeamRecentStats {
  wins: number; losses: number;
  l5Record: string; l10Record: string;
  ppgScored: number; ppgAllowed: number;
  homeRecord?: string; awayRecord?: string;
  atsRecord?: string;
  recentScores: number[];
  l10Wins?: number; l10Losses?: number;
  homeWins?: number; homeLosses?: number;
  awayWins?: number; awayLosses?: number;
  recentPPG?: number;
  pointDiff?: number;
  restDays?: number;
  strengthOfSchedule?: number;
  netRating?: number;
}

async function fetchTeamRecentStats(teamName: string): Promise<TeamRecentStats | null> {
  try {
    const searchName = teamName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z ]/g, "");
    const abbrev = TEAM_ABBREV[searchName];
    if (!abbrev) { console.log("[team-stats] No abbrev for: " + teamName); return null; }
    
    const teamId = TEAM_ESPN_IDS[abbrev] ?? "";
    if (!teamId) { console.log("[team-stats] No ESPN ID for: " + abbrev); return null; }
    
    const teamUrl = `https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/basketball/nba/teams/${teamId}`;
    const teamRes = await quietFetch(teamUrl);
    let wins = 0, losses = 0, ppgScored = 0, ppgAllowed = 0;
    
    if (teamRes.ok && teamRes.text) {
      try {
        const teamData = JSON.parse(teamRes.text);
        const record = teamData.team?.record?.items?.[0]?.summary ?? "";
        if (record) {
          const parts = record.split("-");
          wins = parseInt(parts[0]) || 0;
          losses = parseInt(parts[1]) || 0;
        }
        console.log("[team-stats] " + teamName + " (" + abbrev + "): " + wins + "-" + losses);
      } catch (e) { console.log("[team-stats] Parse error: " + e); }
    }
    
    const statsUrl = `https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/basketball/nba/teams/${teamId}/statistics`;
    const statsRes = await quietFetch(statsUrl);
    if (statsRes.ok && statsRes.text) {
      try {
        const statsData = JSON.parse(statsRes.text);
        const statCategories = statsData.results?.stats?.categories ?? statsData.stats?.categories ?? [];
        for (const category of Array.isArray(statCategories) ? statCategories : []) {
          for (const stat of category.stats ?? []) {
            const sName = stat.name ?? "";
            const sVal = parseFloat(stat.value ?? stat.displayValue ?? "0") || 0;
            if (sName === "avgPoints") ppgScored = sVal;
          }
        }
        if (ppgScored > 0) console.log("[team-stats] " + teamName + " PPG=" + ppgScored.toFixed(1));
      } catch (e) { console.log("[team-stats] Stats parse error: " + e); }
    }

    // Fetch recent games from BDL for advanced stats
    let l10Wins = 0, l10Losses = 0, homeWins = 0, homeLosses = 0, awayWins = 0, awayLosses = 0;
    let recentPPG = 0, pointDiff = 0, restDays = 1;
    const recentScores: number[] = [];
    try {
      const bdlId = bdlTeamNameToId.get(searchName);
      if (bdlId && BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY) {
        const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 30*24*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10);
        const gamesUrl = "https://api.balldontlie.io/v1/games?team_ids[]=" + bdlId + "&seasons[]=2025&start_date=" + thirtyDaysAgo + "&per_page=25";
        const gamesRes = await fetch(gamesUrl, { headers: { "Authorization": BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY } });
        if (gamesRes.ok) {
          const gamesData = await gamesRes.json();
          const games = (gamesData.data || []).filter((g: any) => g.status === "Final");
          games.sort((a: any, b: any) => new Date(b.date).getTime() - new Date(a.date).getTime());
          if (games.length > 0) {
            const lastGameDate = new Date(games[0].date);
            restDays = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - lastGameDate.getTime()) / (1000*60*60*24)));
          }
          const last10 = games.slice(0, 10);
          for (const g of last10) {
            const isHome = g.home_team?.id === bdlId;
            const ts = isHome ? g.home_team_score : g.visitor_team_score;
            const os = isHome ? g.visitor_team_score : g.home_team_score;
            if (ts > os) l10Wins++; else l10Losses++;
          }
          const last5 = games.slice(0, 5);
          let l5Total = 0;
          for (const g of last5) {
            const isHome = g.home_team?.id === bdlId;
            const ts = isHome ? g.home_team_score : g.visitor_team_score;
            l5Total += ts;
            recentScores.push(ts);
          }
          recentPPG = last5.length > 0 ? l5Total / last5.length : 0;
          let totalPD = 0;
          for (const g of games) {
            const isHome = g.home_team?.id === bdlId;
            const ts = isHome ? g.home_team_score : g.visitor_team_score;
            const os = isHome ? g.visitor_team_score : g.home_team_score;
            totalPD += (ts - os);
            if (isHome) { if (ts > os) homeWins++; else homeLosses++; }
            else { if (ts > os) awayWins++; else awayLosses++; }
          }
          pointDiff = games.length > 0 ? totalPD / games.length : 0;
          console.log("[team-stats-bdl] " + teamName + " | L10=" + l10Wins + "-" + l10Losses + " | Home=" + homeWins + "-" + homeLosses + " | Away=" + awayWins + "-" + awayLosses + " | L5PPG=" + recentPPG.toFixed(1) + " | PtDiff=" + pointDiff.toFixed(1) + " | Rest=" + restDays + "d");
        }
      }
    } catch (bdlErr) { console.log("[team-stats-bdl] BDL error: " + bdlErr); }

    // GOAT-ready: Try BDL team season averages for net rating (requires GOAT tier)
    let netRating = ppgScored - ppgAllowed; // ESPN fallback
    let strengthOfSchedule = 0.5; // neutral default
    try {
      const bdlId2 = bdlTeamNameToId.get(searchName);
      if (bdlId2 && BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY) {
        // Try GOAT-only endpoints (401 on ALL-STAR, auto-works after upgrade)
        const advUrl = "https://api.balldontlie.io/v1/teamseasonaverages/advanced?season=2025&team_id=" + bdlId2;
        const advRes = await fetch(advUrl, { headers: { "Authorization": BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY } });
        if (advRes.ok) {
          const advData = await advRes.json();
          if (advData.data?.[0]) {
            netRating = advData.data[0].net_rating ?? netRating;
            console.log("[team-stats-goat] " + teamName + " netRating=" + netRating);
          }
        } else {
          console.log("[team-stats-goat] Advanced stats unavailable (tier): using ESPN fallback netRating=" + netRating.toFixed(1));
        }

        // Try standings for SOS (GOAT-only)
        const standUrl = "https://api.balldontlie.io/v1/standings?season=2025";
        const standRes = await fetch(standUrl, { headers: { "Authorization": BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY } });
        if (standRes.ok) {
          const standData = await standRes.json();
          const standMap = new Map<number, number>();
          for (const s of standData.data || []) {
            const gp = (s.wins || 0) + (s.losses || 0);
            standMap.set(s.team?.id || 0, gp > 0 ? s.wins / gp : 0.5);
          }
          // Calculate SOS from recent opponents
          const thirtyDaysAgo2 = new Date(Date.now() - 30*24*60*60*1000).toISOString().slice(0,10);
          const sosUrl = "https://api.balldontlie.io/v1/games?team_ids[]=" + bdlId2 + "&seasons[]=2025&start_date=" + thirtyDaysAgo2 + "&per_page=15";
          const sosRes = await fetch(sosUrl, { headers: { "Authorization": BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY } });
          if (sosRes.ok) {
            const sosData = await sosRes.json();
            const finals = (sosData.data || []).filter((g: any) => g.status === "Final");
            let oppWpSum = 0, oppCount = 0;
            for (const g of finals) {
              const isHome = g.home_team?.id === bdlId2;
              const oppId = isHome ? g.visitor_team?.id : g.home_team?.id;
              const oppWp = standMap.get(oppId);
              if (oppWp !== undefined) { oppWpSum += oppWp; oppCount++; }
            }
            if (oppCount > 0) strengthOfSchedule = oppWpSum / oppCount;
          }
          console.log("[team-stats-goat] " + teamName + " SOS=" + strengthOfSchedule.toFixed(3));
        } else {
          console.log("[team-stats-goat] Standings unavailable (tier): SOS=0.5 (neutral)");
        }
      }
    } catch (goatErr) { console.log("[team-stats-goat] Error: " + goatErr); }
    return { wins, losses, l5Record: l10Wins+"-"+l10Losses, l10Record: l10Wins+"-"+l10Losses, ppgScored, ppgAllowed, recentScores, l10Wins, l10Losses, homeWins, homeLosses, awayWins, awayLosses, recentPPG, pointDiff, restDays, strengthOfSchedule, netRating };
  } catch (e) {
    console.log("[team-stats] Error: " + e);
    return null;
  }
}

// ESPN Team IDs for stat lookups
const TEAM_ESPN_IDS: Record<string, string> = {
  "ATL": "1", "BOS": "2", "BKN": "17", "CHA": "30", "CHI": "4",
  "CLE": "5", "DAL": "6", "DEN": "7", "DET": "8", "GS": "9", "GSW": "9",
  "HOU": "10", "IND": "11", "LAC": "12", "LAL": "13", "MEM": "29",
  "MIA": "14", "MIL": "15", "MIN": "16", "NO": "3", "NOP": "3", "NY": "18", "NYK": "18",
  "OKC": "25", "ORL": "19", "PHI": "20", "PHX": "21", "POR": "22",
  "SA": "24", "SAS": "24", "SAC": "23", "TOR": "28", "UTA": "26", "WAS": "27",
};

function scoreGameSide(
  game: GameOdds,
  homeStats: TeamRecentStats | null,
  awayStats: TeamRecentStats | null,
  homeInjuries: string[],
  awayInjuries: string[],
  h2h?: { homeWins: number; awayWins: number }
): { side: string; team: string; spread: number; odds: number; confidence: number; breakdown: Record<string, number> } | null {
  if (!game.spread) return null;
  
  let score = 50;
  const breakdown: Record<string, number> = {};
  
  // Factor 1: Win % differential
  let homeWinPct = homeStats ? homeStats.wins / Math.max(1, homeStats.wins + homeStats.losses) : 0.5;
  let awayWinPct = awayStats ? awayStats.wins / Math.max(1, awayStats.wins + awayStats.losses) : 0.5;
  const winPctDiff = homeWinPct - awayWinPct;
  const winPctScore = Math.round(winPctDiff * 20); // +/-10 range
  breakdown.winPctDiff = winPctScore;
  
  // Factor 2: PPG differential vs spread
  const homePPG = homeStats?.ppgScored ?? 0;
  const awayPPG = awayStats?.ppgScored ?? 0;
  const homeDefPPG = homeStats?.ppgAllowed ?? 0;
  const awayDefPPG = awayStats?.ppgAllowed ?? 0;
  
  // Expected margin: (home offense - away defense) - (away offense - home defense) + home court advantage (~3pts)
  const expectedMargin = ((homePPG - awayDefPPG) - (awayPPG - homeDefPPG)) / 2 + 3;
  // spreadEdge: does expected margin beat the spread?
  // Favored (spread=-5, margin=10): 10+(-5)=5 edge. Underdog (spread=+5, margin=-2): -2+5=3 edge.
  const spreadEdge = expectedMargin + game.spread.homeSpread;
  const spreadScore = spreadEdge > 5 ? 10 : spreadEdge > 3 ? 7 : spreadEdge > 1 ? 4 : spreadEdge > 0 ? 2 : spreadEdge > -2 ? -2 : spreadEdge > -4 ? -5 : -8;
  breakdown.spreadEdge = spreadScore;
  
  // Factor 3: Injury impact — count significant injuries per team
  const homeInjuryCount = homeInjuries.filter(i => i.includes("Out") || i.includes("Day-to-Day")).length;
  const awayInjuryCount = awayInjuries.filter(i => i.includes("Out") || i.includes("Day-to-Day")).length;
  const injuryDiff = awayInjuryCount - homeInjuryCount; // positive = away team more injured = good for home
  const injuryScore = injuryDiff >= 3 ? 6 : injuryDiff >= 1 ? 3 : injuryDiff <= -3 ? -6 : injuryDiff <= -1 ? -3 : 0;
  breakdown.injuryImpact = injuryScore;
  
  // Factor 4: Home court advantage
  breakdown.homeCourt = 2;

  // Factor 5: L10 record differential
  const homeL10WP = (homeStats?.l10Wins ?? 5) / Math.max(1, (homeStats?.l10Wins ?? 5) + (homeStats?.l10Losses ?? 5));
  const awayL10WP = (awayStats?.l10Wins ?? 5) / Math.max(1, (awayStats?.l10Wins ?? 5) + (awayStats?.l10Losses ?? 5));
  const l10Score = Math.round((homeL10WP - awayL10WP) * 12);
  breakdown.l10Form = l10Score;

  // Factor 6: Home/Away record advantage
  const homeHWP = (homeStats?.homeWins ?? 0) / Math.max(1, (homeStats?.homeWins ?? 0) + (homeStats?.homeLosses ?? 1));
  const awayAWP = (awayStats?.awayWins ?? 0) / Math.max(1, (awayStats?.awayWins ?? 0) + (awayStats?.awayLosses ?? 1));
  const haScore = Math.round((homeHWP - awayAWP) * 8);
  breakdown.homeAwayRecord = haScore;

  // Factor 7: Net point differential
  const pdDiff = (homeStats?.pointDiff ?? 0) - (awayStats?.pointDiff ?? 0);
  const pdScore = pdDiff > 8 ? 8 : pdDiff > 4 ? 5 : pdDiff > 2 ? 3 : pdDiff > 0 ? 1 : pdDiff > -2 ? -1 : pdDiff > -4 ? -3 : pdDiff > -8 ? -5 : -8;
  breakdown.pointDiffEdge = pdScore;

  // Factor 8: Rest advantage
  const restDiff = (homeStats?.restDays ?? 1) - (awayStats?.restDays ?? 1);
  const restScore = restDiff >= 3 ? 5 : restDiff >= 1 ? 2 : restDiff <= -3 ? -5 : restDiff <= -1 ? -2 : 0;
  breakdown.restAdvantage = restScore;

  // Factor 9: Scoring trend (L5 PPG vs season PPG)
  const homeTrend = homeStats?.recentPPG && homeStats?.ppgScored ? (homeStats.recentPPG - homeStats.ppgScored) : 0;
  const awayTrend = awayStats?.recentPPG && awayStats?.ppgScored ? (awayStats.recentPPG - awayStats.ppgScored) : 0;
  const trendDiff = homeTrend - awayTrend;
  const trendScore = trendDiff > 6 ? 3 : trendDiff > 3 ? 2 : trendDiff < -6 ? -3 : trendDiff < -3 ? -2 : 0;
  breakdown.scoringTrend = trendScore;

  // Factor 10: Head-to-head this season
  const h2hDiff = (h2h?.homeWins ?? 0) - (h2h?.awayWins ?? 0);
  const h2hScore = h2hDiff >= 2 ? 4 : h2hDiff >= 1 ? 2 : h2hDiff <= -2 ? -4 : h2hDiff <= -1 ? -2 : 0;
  breakdown.headToHead = h2hScore;

  // Factor 11: Strength of schedule (GOAT-ready, neutral until upgraded)
  const homeSOS = homeStats?.strengthOfSchedule ?? 0.5;
  const awaySOS = awayStats?.strengthOfSchedule ?? 0.5;
  const sosDiff = homeSOS - awaySOS;
  const sosScore = sosDiff > 0.05 ? 3 : sosDiff > 0.02 ? 1 : sosDiff < -0.05 ? -3 : sosDiff < -0.02 ? -1 : 0;
  breakdown.strengthOfSchedule = sosScore;

  // Factor 12: Net rating differential (GOAT uses BDL advanced, fallback uses PPG diff)
  const homeNR = homeStats?.netRating ?? 0;
  const awayNR = awayStats?.netRating ?? 0;
  const nrDiff = homeNR - awayNR;
  const nrScore = nrDiff > 8 ? 6 : nrDiff > 4 ? 4 : nrDiff > 2 ? 2 : nrDiff > 0 ? 1 : nrDiff > -2 ? -1 : nrDiff > -4 ? -2 : nrDiff > -8 ? -4 : -6;
  breakdown.netRating = nrScore;

  score += winPctScore + spreadScore + injuryScore + 2 + l10Score + haScore + pdScore + restScore + trendScore + h2hScore + sosScore + nrScore;
  score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
  
  // Decide which side to pick
  const pickHome = score >= 50;
  
  return {
    side: pickHome ? "home" : "away",
    team: pickHome ? game.homeTeam : game.awayTeam,
    spread: pickHome ? game.spread.homeSpread : game.spread.awaySpread,
    odds: pickHome ? game.spread.homeOdds : game.spread.awayOdds,
    confidence: pickHome ? score : 100 - score,
    breakdown,
  };
}

function scoreGameTotal(
  game: GameOdds,
  homeStats: TeamRecentStats | null,
  awayStats: TeamRecentStats | null
): { side: "over" | "under"; total: number; odds: number; confidence: number; breakdown: Record<string, number> } | null {
  if (!game.total) return null;
  
  let score = 50;
  const breakdown: Record<string, number> = {};
  
  // Factor 1: Combined PPG vs total line
  const combinedPPG = (homeStats?.ppgScored ?? 0) + (awayStats?.ppgScored ?? 0);
  const combinedDefPPG = (homeStats?.ppgAllowed ?? 0) + (awayStats?.ppgAllowed ?? 0);
  const expectedTotal = (combinedPPG + combinedDefPPG) / 2;
  const totalEdge = expectedTotal - game.total.line;
  const totalScore = totalEdge > 8 ? 10 : totalEdge > 4 ? 7 : totalEdge > 2 ? 4 : totalEdge > 0 ? 2 : totalEdge > -2 ? -2 : totalEdge > -4 ? -5 : -8;
  breakdown.totalEdge = totalScore;
  
  // Factor 2: Pace — high-scoring teams push overs
  const avgPPG = ((homeStats?.ppgScored ?? 0) + (awayStats?.ppgScored ?? 0)) / 2;
  const paceScore = avgPPG > 115 ? 5 : avgPPG > 110 ? 3 : avgPPG < 105 ? -3 : avgPPG < 100 ? -5 : 0;
  breakdown.pace = paceScore;
  
  // Factor 3: Defensive matchup
  const avgDefPPG = ((homeStats?.ppgAllowed ?? 0) + (awayStats?.ppgAllowed ?? 0)) / 2;
  const defScore = avgDefPPG > 115 ? 5 : avgDefPPG > 112 ? 3 : avgDefPPG < 108 ? -3 : avgDefPPG < 105 ? -5 : 0;
  breakdown.defense = defScore;
  
  // Factor 4: Scoring trend
  const homeTTrend = homeStats?.recentPPG && homeStats?.ppgScored ? (homeStats.recentPPG - homeStats.ppgScored) : 0;
  const awayTTrend = awayStats?.recentPPG && awayStats?.ppgScored ? (awayStats.recentPPG - awayStats.ppgScored) : 0;
  const combTrend = homeTTrend + awayTTrend;
  const trendTScore = combTrend > 8 ? 5 : combTrend > 4 ? 3 : combTrend > 0 ? 1 : combTrend > -4 ? -1 : combTrend > -8 ? -3 : -5;
  breakdown.scoringTrend = trendTScore;

  // Factor 5: Rest impact on scoring
  const homeRD = homeStats?.restDays ?? 2;
  const awayRD = awayStats?.restDays ?? 2;
  const avgRest = (homeRD + awayRD) / 2;
  const restTScore = avgRest <= 1 ? -3 : avgRest >= 3 ? 2 : 0;
  breakdown.restImpact = restTScore;

  // Factor 6: Net point differential
  const combPD = (homeStats?.pointDiff ?? 0) + (awayStats?.pointDiff ?? 0);
  const pdTScore = combPD > 10 ? 3 : combPD > 5 ? 2 : combPD < -10 ? -3 : combPD < -5 ? -2 : 0;
  breakdown.pointDiffTotal = pdTScore;

  // Factor 7: L10 combined wins
  const combL10W = (homeStats?.l10Wins ?? 5) + (awayStats?.l10Wins ?? 5);
  const l10TScore = combL10W >= 14 ? 3 : combL10W >= 12 ? 1 : combL10W <= 6 ? -3 : combL10W <= 8 ? -1 : 0;
  breakdown.l10Scoring = l10TScore;

  // Factor 8: Strength of schedule (GOAT-ready)
  const homeTSOS = homeStats?.strengthOfSchedule ?? 0.5;
  const awayTSOS = awayStats?.strengthOfSchedule ?? 0.5;
  const avgSOS = (homeTSOS + awayTSOS) / 2;
  const sosTScore = avgSOS > 0.55 ? -2 : avgSOS < 0.45 ? 2 : 0;
  breakdown.scheduleStrength = sosTScore;

  // Factor 9: Combined net rating (high = good teams = could go either way, low = bad teams = likely under)
  const homeNRT = homeStats?.netRating ?? 0;
  const awayNRT = awayStats?.netRating ?? 0;
  const combNR = homeNRT + awayNRT;
  const nrTScore = combNR > 10 ? 3 : combNR > 5 ? 1 : combNR < -10 ? -3 : combNR < -5 ? -1 : 0;
  breakdown.netRatingTotal = nrTScore;

  score += totalScore + paceScore + defScore + trendTScore + restTScore + pdTScore + l10TScore + sosTScore + nrTScore;
  score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
  
  // Decide over or under
  const pickOver = score >= 50;
  
  return {
    side: pickOver ? "over" : "under",
    total: game.total.line,
    odds: pickOver ? game.total.overOdds : game.total.underOdds,
    confidence: pickOver ? score : 100 - score,
    breakdown,
  };
}

// scoreAllGames removed — superseded by inline game section


Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  // Quick test — remove after debugging
  try { const u = new URL(req.url); if (u.searchParams.get("ping") === "1") return new Response(JSON.stringify({pong:true}), {headers:{...corsHeaders,"Content-Type":"application/json"}}); if (u.searchParams.get("step")) { const step = u.searchParams.get("step"); if (step === "weights") { await loadWeightsFromDB(); return new Response(JSON.stringify({step:"weights",ok:true}), {headers:{...corsHeaders,"Content-Type":"application/json"}}); } if (step === "phase2") { await loadWeightsFromDB(); let dateOffset=0; try{const b=await req.json();if(b?.dateOffset===1)dateOffset=1;}catch(e){} const odds=await fetchTodaysProps(dateOffset); await loadBdlInjuries(); return new Response(JSON.stringify({step:"phase2",events:odds.events.length,props:odds.allProps.length,bdlTeams:bdlTeamNameToId.size}), {headers:{...corsHeaders,"Content-Type":"application/json"}}); } if (step === "odds") { await loadWeightsFromDB(); let dateOffset=0; try{const b=await req.json();if(b?.dateOffset===1)dateOffset=1;}catch(e){} const odds=await fetchTodaysProps(dateOffset); return new Response(JSON.stringify({step:"odds",events:odds.events.length,props:odds.allProps.length}), {headers:{...corsHeaders,"Content-Type":"application/json"}}); } } } catch(e) {}
  // Load dynamic weights from DB
  await loadWeightsFromDB();
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  try {
    console.log("\n========================================");
    console.log("=== GET RECOMMENDATIONS (Monolithic) ===");
    resetRunStats();
    console.log("========================================\n");
    const startTime = Date.now();
    let timedOut = false;

    // Phase 1: Fetch all games and props from Odds API
    console.log("[PHASE 1] Fetching props from The Odds API...");
    const phase1Start = Date.now();
    // Parse date offset from request body (0=today, 1=tomorrow)
    let dateOffset = 0;
    try {
      const body = await req.json();
      if (body?.dateOffset === 1) dateOffset = 1;
    } catch (parseErr) {
      // No body or invalid JSON — default to today
    }
    console.log("[recs] Date offset: " + dateOffset + " (" + (dateOffset === 0 ? "today" : "tomorrow") + ")");
    const { events, allProps } = await fetchTodaysProps(dateOffset);
    const phase1Time = ((Date.now() - phase1Start) / 1000).toFixed(1);

    console.log(`[PHASE 1 COMPLETE] Fetched ${allProps.length} props from ${events.length} games in ${phase1Time}s\n`);
    runStats.gamesFound = events.length;
    runStats.propsFetched = allProps.length;

    if (!events.length) {
      return jsonResponse({
        success: true,
        complete: true,
        generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        gamesAnalyzed: 0,
        propsAnalyzed: 0,
        recommendations: [],
        message: "No NBA games today",
      });
    }

    if (!allProps.length) {
      return jsonResponse({
        success: true,
        complete: true,
        generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        gamesAnalyzed: events.length,
        propsAnalyzed: 0,
        recommendations: [],
        message: "No player props available yet",
      });
    }

    // Cap props to prevent Supabase Free tier timeout (150s limit)
    if (allProps.length > 100) { console.log("[odds] Capping from " + allProps.length + " to 100 props"); allProps.splice(100); }

    // Phase 2: Group props by player and fetch ESPN data
    console.log("[PHASE 2] Fetching ESPN data for all players...");
    const phase2Start = Date.now();

    // Group props by player
    const propsByPlayer = new Map<string, ExtractedProp[]>();
    for (const prop of allProps) {
      const key = prop.playerName.toLowerCase();
      if (!propsByPlayer.has(key)) propsByPlayer.set(key, []);
      propsByPlayer.get(key)!.push(prop);
    }

    const uniquePlayers = Array.from(propsByPlayer.keys());
    console.log(`[PHASE 2] ${uniquePlayers.length} unique players to fetch`);

    // Load BDL data once (populates bdlTeamNameToId for game scoring)
    await loadBdlInjuries();

    // Fetch ESPN data for all players (10 concurrent)
    const playerDataCache = new Map<string, CachedPlayerData | null>();
    let playersLoaded = 0;
    let playersSkipped = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < uniquePlayers.length; i += PLAYER_BATCH_SIZE) {
      // Check timeout
      if (Date.now() - startTime > TIMEOUT_MS - 60000) { // Leave 60s for scoring and Gemini
        console.log(`[PHASE 2] Timeout approaching, stopping ESPN fetches at ${i}/${uniquePlayers.length}`);
        timedOut = true;
        break;
      }

      const batch = uniquePlayers.slice(i, i + PLAYER_BATCH_SIZE);
      const batchNum = Math.floor(i / PLAYER_BATCH_SIZE) + 1;
      const totalBatches = Math.ceil(uniquePlayers.length / PLAYER_BATCH_SIZE);
      console.log(`[PHASE 2] Batch ${batchNum}/${totalBatches}: ${batch.length} players...`);

      const results = await Promise.all(batch.map(async (playerKey) => {
        const playerProps = propsByPlayer.get(playerKey)!;
        const playerName = playerProps[0].playerName;
        try {
          const data = await fetchPlayerDataCached(playerName);
          playerDataCache.set(playerKey, data);
          return data ? { success: true } : { success: false };
        } catch (parseErr) {
          playerDataCache.set(playerKey, null);
          return { success: false };
        }
      }));

      for (const r of results) {
        if (r.success) playersLoaded++;
        else playersSkipped++;
      }
    }

    const phase2Time = ((Date.now() - phase2Start) / 1000).toFixed(1);
    console.log(`[PHASE 2 COMPLETE] Loaded ${playersLoaded} players, skipped ${playersSkipped} in ${phase2Time}s\n`);
    runStats.playersLoaded = playersLoaded;
    runStats.playersSkipped = playersSkipped;

    // Phase 3: Fetch team edge data (B2B, opponent stats)
    console.log("[PHASE 3] Fetching team edge data...");
    const phase3Start = Date.now();

    // Get unique teams from props
    const uniqueTeams = new Set<string>();
    for (const prop of allProps) {
      uniqueTeams.add(prop.homeTeam);
      uniqueTeams.add(prop.awayTeam);
    }

    const teamEdgeCache = new Map<string, { b2b: { isBackToBack: boolean; restDays: number }; oppStats: OpponentStats | null }>();

    // Fetch scoreboard ONCE for all opponent ID lookups (deduplicates ~30 fetches to 1)
    const scoreboardData = await fetchScoreboard();
    if (!scoreboardData) {
      console.log("[PHASE 3] WARNING: Failed to fetch scoreboard - opponent stats will be unavailable");
    }

    // Fetch B2B and opponent IDs for all teams
    const teamNames = Array.from(uniqueTeams);
    const b2bResults = await Promise.all(teamNames.map(team => checkBackToBack(team)));
    const oppIdResults = teamNames.map(team =>
      scoreboardData ? fetchOpponentTeamIdFromScoreboard(team, scoreboardData) : null
    );

    // Create a map of team -> opponent ID
    const teamToOppId = new Map<string, string | null>();
    for (let i = 0; i < teamNames.length; i++) {
      teamToOppId.set(teamNames[i], oppIdResults[i]);
    }

    // Fetch opponent stats for all opponent IDs
    const uniqueOppIds = [...new Set(oppIdResults.filter(id => id !== null) as string[])];
    const oppStatsResults = await Promise.all(uniqueOppIds.map(id => fetchOpponentDefensiveStats(id)));
    const oppIdToStats = new Map<string, OpponentStats | null>();
    for (let i = 0; i < uniqueOppIds.length; i++) {
      oppIdToStats.set(uniqueOppIds[i], oppStatsResults[i]);
    }

    // Build team edge cache (normalize team names for consistent lookup)
    for (let i = 0; i < teamNames.length; i++) {
      const team = teamNames[i];
      const normalizedTeam = normalizeTeamName(team);
      const b2b = b2bResults[i];
      const oppId = teamToOppId.get(team);
      const oppStats = oppId ? oppIdToStats.get(oppId) ?? null : null;
      if (oppStats) {
        teamEdgeCache.set(normalizedTeam, { b2b, oppStats });
      } else {
        console.log(`[oppStats-warn] NULL oppStats for ${team} — ESPN fetch failed even after retry`);
        // Still store in cache with null oppStats so players get default 0 for defense scores
        teamEdgeCache.set(normalizedTeam, { b2b, oppStats: null });
      }
    }

    const phase3Time = ((Date.now() - phase3Start) / 1000).toFixed(1);
    for (const [, edgeEntry] of teamEdgeCache) { if (edgeEntry.oppStats) runStats.oppStatsFound++; else runStats.oppStatsFailed++; }
    console.log(`[PHASE 3 COMPLETE] Fetched edge data for ${uniqueTeams.size} teams in ${phase3Time}s\n`);

    // Phase 4: Score ALL props
    console.log("[PHASE 4] Scoring all props...");
    const phase4Start = Date.now();

    const allResults: Array<{ result: PropAnalysisResult; prop: ExtractedProp }> = [];

    for (const [playerKey, playerProps] of propsByPlayer) {
      const playerData = playerDataCache.get(playerKey);
      if (!playerData) continue;

      // Get team and opponent info from the first prop (from Odds API)
      const firstProp = playerProps[0];
      const team = firstProp.homeTeam === playerData.player.team || normalizeTeamName(firstProp.homeTeam) === normalizeTeamName(playerData.player.team)
        ? firstProp.homeTeam
        : firstProp.awayTeam;
      const opponent = team === firstProp.homeTeam ? firstProp.awayTeam : firstProp.homeTeam;



      // Determine team for edge data lookup (normalize for consistent matching)
      const rawTeam = playerData.player.team;
      const teamKey = normalizeTeamName(rawTeam);
      const edgeData = teamEdgeCache.get(teamKey) ?? { b2b: { isBackToBack: false, restDays: 1 }, oppStats: null };




      for (const prop of playerProps) {
        const result = await scoreProp(playerData, prop, edgeData);
        if (result) {
          console.log(`[score] ${result.playerName} | ${result.propType} ${result.pickSide} ${result.line} | confidence=${result.confidence} | ${JSON.stringify(result.breakdown)}`);
          // Push raw result - context modifiers will be applied in Phase 5 for recommendations only
          allResults.push({ result, prop });
          // Log to pick_history with raw confidence (recommendations will be re-logged in Phase 5 with adjusted confidence)
          const isRecommended = result.confidence >= 70;
          if (!isRecommended) {
            // Only log non-recommendations here; recommendations will be logged in Phase 5 with adjusted confidence
            logPickToHistory(result, prop, false, null);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // === GAME SIDES & TOTALS ===
    try {
      const gameDate = (() => { const n = new Date(); const e = new Date(n.getTime() - 4*60*60*1000); return e.toISOString().slice(0,10).replace(/-/g,""); })();
      const logUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") + "/rest/v1/pick_history";
      const logKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") || "";
      let gamePickCount = 0;

      // Cache team stats to avoid duplicate fetches (same team appears in spread + total)
      const teamStatsCache = new Map<string, TeamRecentStats | null>();
      async function getCachedTeamStats(teamName: string): Promise<TeamRecentStats | null> {
        const key = teamName.toLowerCase();
        if (teamStatsCache.has(key)) return teamStatsCache.get(key)!;
        const stats = await fetchTeamRecentStats(teamName);
        teamStatsCache.set(key, stats);
        return stats;
      }

      // Populate gameOddsCache if empty (needed when props come from DB cache)
      if (gameOddsCache.size === 0 && events.length > 0) {
        // Circuit breaker: skip game-lines fetches when credits are low. gameOddsCache
        // stays empty and the downstream loop will skip games that lack odds data.
        const cbOkGameLines = await checkCircuitBreaker({ source: "game-lines", event_count: events.length });
        if (!cbOkGameLines) {
          console.log("[game] Circuit breaker tripped — skipping spreads/totals fetches");
        } else {
          console.log("[game] gameOddsCache empty — fetching spreads/totals from Odds API...");
          for (const event of events) {
            try {
              const url = "https://api.the-odds-api.com/v4/sports/basketball_nba/events/" + event.id + "/odds?apiKey=" + ODDS_API_KEY + "&regions=us,us2&markets=spreads,totals&oddsFormat=american&bookmakers=hardrockbet,draftkings,fanduel";
              let res: { ok: boolean; status: number; text: string } = { ok: false, status: 0, text: "" };
              try {
                const rawRes = await fetch(url);
                const rawText = await rawRes.text();
                res = { ok: rawRes.ok, status: rawRes.status, text: rawText };
                await logApiUsage("event_odds_game_lines", rawRes.status, rawRes.headers, { event_id: event.id, home: event.home_team, away: event.away_team });
              } catch (fetchErr) {
                await logApiUsage("event_odds_game_lines", 0, null, { event_id: event.id, error: String(fetchErr) });
              }
              if (res.ok && res.text) {
                const data = JSON.parse(res.text);
                gameOddsCache.set(event.id, data);
              }
            } catch (e) { console.log("[game] Odds fetch error: " + e); }
          }
          console.log("[game] Loaded spreads/totals for " + gameOddsCache.size + " events");
        }
      }

      for (const event of events) {
        const eventData = gameOddsCache.get(event.id);
        if (!eventData) { console.log("[game] No odds data for " + event.home_team + " id=" + event.id); continue; }
        try {
          const hrb = (eventData.bookmakers ?? []).find((b: any) => b.key === "hardrockbet") || (eventData.bookmakers ?? [])[0];
          if (!hrb) { console.log("[game] No bookmaker for " + event.home_team); continue; }
          
          let spreadLine = 0, spreadOdds = -110;
          let totalLine = 0, totalOverOdds = -110, totalUnderOdds = -110;
          
          for (const mkt of hrb.markets ?? []) {
            if (mkt.key === "spreads") {
              const home = (mkt.outcomes ?? []).find((o: any) => o.name === event.home_team);
              if (home) { spreadLine = home.point; spreadOdds = home.price; }
            }
            if (mkt.key === "totals") {
              const over = (mkt.outcomes ?? []).find((o: any) => o.name === "Over");
              const under = (mkt.outcomes ?? []).find((o: any) => o.name === "Under");
              if (over) { totalLine = over.point; totalOverOdds = over.price; }
              if (under) { totalUnderOdds = under.price; }
            }
          }
          
          console.log("[game] " + event.away_team + " @ " + event.home_team + " spread=" + spreadLine + " total=" + totalLine);
          
          // Fetch team stats ONCE for both spread and total scoring
          const homeStats = await getCachedTeamStats(event.home_team);
          const awayStats = await getCachedTeamStats(event.away_team);
          if (!homeStats || !awayStats) {
            console.log("[game] Stats missing: home=" + (homeStats ? "OK" : "NULL") + " away=" + (awayStats ? "OK" : "NULL"));
          }
          
          // Fetch H2H from BDL games (both teams)
          let h2hHomeWins = 0, h2hAwayWins = 0;
          try {
            const homeBdlId = bdlTeamNameToId.get(event.home_team.toLowerCase());
            const awayBdlId = bdlTeamNameToId.get(event.away_team.toLowerCase());
            if (homeBdlId && awayBdlId && BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY) {
              const h2hUrl = "https://api.balldontlie.io/v1/games?team_ids[]=" + homeBdlId + "&team_ids[]=" + awayBdlId + "&seasons[]=2025&per_page=10";
              const h2hRes = await fetch(h2hUrl, { headers: { "Authorization": BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY } });
              if (h2hRes.ok) {
                const h2hData = await h2hRes.json();
                for (const g of (h2hData.data || []).filter((g: any) => g.status === "Final")) {
                  const homeWon = g.home_team?.id === homeBdlId ? g.home_team_score > g.visitor_team_score : g.visitor_team_score > g.home_team_score;
                  if (homeWon) h2hHomeWins++; else h2hAwayWins++;
                }
                console.log("[game-h2h] " + event.home_team + " vs " + event.away_team + " H2H=" + h2hHomeWins + "-" + h2hAwayWins);
              }
            }
          } catch (h2hErr) { console.log("[game-h2h] Error: " + h2hErr); }

          if (spreadLine !== 0 && homeStats && awayStats) {
              const sideResult = scoreGameSide(
                { eventId: event.id, homeTeam: event.home_team, awayTeam: event.away_team, gameTime: event.commence_time,
                  spread: { homeSpread: spreadLine, homeOdds: spreadOdds, awaySpread: -spreadLine, awayOdds: -spreadOdds } },
                homeStats, awayStats, [], [], { homeWins: h2hHomeWins, awayWins: h2hAwayWins }
              );
              if (sideResult) {
                const sbd = sideResult.breakdown || {};
                const row = { player_name: sideResult.team, team: sideResult.team, opponent: sideResult.side === "home" ? event.away_team : event.home_team,
                  game_time: formatGameTime(event.commence_time), game_date: gameDate, prop_type: "spread", line: sideResult.spread,
                  pick_side: sideResult.side, odds: sideResult.odds, confidence: sideResult.confidence, verdict: getScoreLabel(sideResult.confidence), recommendation_shown: sideResult.confidence >= 70,
                  score_l10_form: sbd.l10Form ?? null, score_ha_record: sbd.homeAwayRecord ?? null, score_point_diff: sbd.pointDiffEdge ?? null,
                  score_rest_advantage: sbd.restAdvantage ?? null, score_scoring_trend: sbd.scoringTrend ?? null, score_h2h: sbd.headToHead ?? null,
                  score_sos: sbd.strengthOfSchedule ?? null, score_net_rating: sbd.netRating ?? null };
                await fetch(logUrl, { method: "POST", headers: { "apikey": logKey, "Authorization": "Bearer " + logKey, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Prefer": "resolution=ignore-duplicates" }, body: JSON.stringify(row) });
                allResults.push({ result: { ...sideResult, playerName: sideResult.team, team: sideResult.team, propType: "spread", line: sideResult.spread, pickSide: sideResult.side, odds: sideResult.odds, confidence: sideResult.confidence, verdict: getScoreLabel(sideResult.confidence), hitRates: {l5:"",l10:"",season:""}, breakdown: sideResult.breakdown, seasonAvg: 0, recentAvg: 0, aiAnalysis: null, opponent: sideResult.side === "home" ? event.away_team : event.home_team, gameTime: formatGameTime(event.commence_time), teamStats: { home: homeStats, away: awayStats, h2h: { homeWins: h2hHomeWins, awayWins: h2hAwayWins } } } as any, prop: { playerName: sideResult.team, propType: "spread", line: sideResult.spread, odds: sideResult.odds, homeTeam: event.home_team, awayTeam: event.away_team, gameTime: event.commence_time } as ExtractedProp });
                gamePickCount++;
                console.log("[game] SPREAD: " + sideResult.team + " " + sideResult.spread + " conf=" + sideResult.confidence);
              }
          }
          
          if (totalLine > 0 && homeStats && awayStats) {
              const totalResult = scoreGameTotal(
                { eventId: event.id, homeTeam: event.home_team, awayTeam: event.away_team, gameTime: event.commence_time,
                  total: { line: totalLine, overOdds: totalOverOdds, underOdds: totalUnderOdds } },
                homeStats, awayStats
              );
              if (totalResult) {
                const matchup = event.home_team + " vs " + event.away_team;
                const tbd = totalResult.breakdown || {};
                const row = { player_name: matchup, team: event.home_team, opponent: event.away_team,
                  game_time: formatGameTime(event.commence_time), game_date: gameDate, prop_type: "game_total", line: totalResult.total,
                  pick_side: totalResult.side, odds: totalResult.odds, confidence: totalResult.confidence, verdict: getScoreLabel(totalResult.confidence), recommendation_shown: totalResult.confidence >= 70,
                  score_l10_form: tbd.l10Scoring ?? null, score_ha_record: null, score_point_diff: tbd.pointDiffTotal ?? null,
                  score_rest_advantage: tbd.restImpact ?? null, score_scoring_trend: tbd.scoringTrend ?? null, score_h2h: null,
                  score_sos: tbd.scheduleStrength ?? null, score_net_rating: tbd.netRatingTotal ?? null };
                await fetch(logUrl, { method: "POST", headers: { "apikey": logKey, "Authorization": "Bearer " + logKey, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Prefer": "resolution=ignore-duplicates" }, body: JSON.stringify(row) });
                allResults.push({ result: { playerName: matchup, team: event.home_team, propType: "game_total", line: totalResult.total, pickSide: totalResult.side, odds: totalResult.odds, confidence: totalResult.confidence, verdict: getScoreLabel(totalResult.confidence), hitRates: {l5:"",l10:"",season:""}, breakdown: totalResult.breakdown, seasonAvg: 0, recentAvg: 0, aiAnalysis: null, opponent: event.away_team, gameTime: formatGameTime(event.commence_time), teamStats: { home: homeStats, away: awayStats, h2h: { homeWins: h2hHomeWins, awayWins: h2hAwayWins } } } as any, prop: { playerName: matchup, propType: "game_total", line: totalResult.total, odds: totalResult.odds, homeTeam: event.home_team, awayTeam: event.away_team, gameTime: event.commence_time } as ExtractedProp });
                gamePickCount++;
                console.log("[game] TOTAL: " + matchup + " " + totalResult.side + " " + totalResult.total + " conf=" + totalResult.confidence);
              }
          }
        } catch (parseErr) { console.log("[game] Error for " + event.home_team + ": " + parseErr); }
      }
      console.log("[game] Completed: " + gamePickCount + " game picks from " + events.length + " events");
    } catch (gameErr) {
      console.log("[game] Error: " + String(gameErr));
    }
